1225|## 6. Interpretation limits
1226|
1227|These registers cover located explicitly recorded deviations, not a forensic guarantee that no other incident exists. Per-result errata/model findings, guard HOLDs, queue supersessions and receipts remain authoritative at their scopes. Mathematical acceptance never certifies process compliance, novelty, runtime safety, human review or a kernel proof. Future activity/settlement require fresh receipts, not this snapshot.

4|
5|## ZERO candidate
6|
7|On the actual residual, equal block labels exist because k>p. Their Φ2,T=0 test admits n=2κ if there are at least2κzero letters: then R≥2κ automatically and2κ≤2p−2. Thus z0≤2κ−1 is valid even when R<2κ (the forbidden antecedent is then impossible).
8|
9|Let δ=p+2κ−k≥0, so R=p+δ. In the lower-half κ≤(p−1)/2, if1≤z0≤δ+1, at leastp−1nonzero rotations remain. A pattern Φ=2κ+2 exists because Φ≤p+1<k; its GRX interval includes every even size0..p−1. For any p−1distinct nonzero signed occurrences, iterated classicalCD on the two-element sets{0,x_i} reaches all of F_p. A representing subset of odd size has at mostp−2terms, so one unused zero letter fixes parity without exceedingp−1. Every target T is attained, contradiction. Therefore z0=0 or z0≥δ+2. At k=p+2, δ=2κ−2, combining both bounds forces z0=0. This excludes zero LETTERS in the named lower-half class; it does **not** prove Y zero-sum-free or exclude the NF family.
10|
11|## TOP candidates: checked arithmetic, not full path proof
12|
13|Use accepted HK1 top near-pair structure, normalize its nonzero a to1, and let D be off-pair letters, S_± signed ±1counts. Then S_++S_-=3p−4−D; for Φ≥4, max(S_+,S_-)≥2p−2 implies |S_+−S_-|≥p+D. If a path P→complement(P) keeps Φ≥4 and changes one run per step, a sign crossing forces |g_q|≥p+D for one run. **The actual five-phase/single-run path construction is not given in cp02 and has not been verified here.** BIG-RUN remains a source/derivation obligation in the final manuscript.
14|
15|Assuming such q with g_q≥p+D, a minus-set not containing q has S_-≤A'+(A−α_q)≤2p−4−2D. For Φ≥4 this forces the heavy sign to be positive, S_+≥2p−2, S_-≤p−2−D; complements reverse signs. All-plus GRX prohibits2pcopiesof+1 (A≤2p−1); because A≥p, it also prohibits p copies of each sign (A'≤p−1).
16|
17|For a balanced Φ2 pattern with S_±≤2p−2−D, choosing ALL D deviant occurrences leaves n'=2p−2−D choices from ±1. The negative-count interval has exactlyp−1integer positions, giving an AP with a unique missing residue T. Dropping one deviant d raises n' by1 and lowers the AP's initial value by d+1; the new AP has p−2positions. Compatibility of the same excluded T forces the signed d to be±1, contrary to deviancy. Hence D≥1 precludes this balance. At D=0 the unique missing residue is T=−2S_-−4. This is an AP-size/signed-count argument at the literal Φ2 size, not a CT extension to signed multisets.
18|
19|If a signed +1value has at least n copies and the number of non-+1occurrences is at mostn, every fixed-size n sum equals n plus an unrestricted subset sum of W={x^z−1:x^z≠1}, with distinct occurrence indexing; pad by available+1copies. The Φ≥4 HK1 heavy bound and n≥p−1 supply those capacities. Without the capacity hypotheses the unrestricted-subset rewrite is not licensed.

471|- Final recorded coverage is GB15/18, not full physical n8. Global runtime HOLD remains. Three native attempt settlements do not imply whole-window/current-process settlement.
472|- These records support an attributed historical report. Raw proof payloads, every source/binary behavior, global safety and original scientific normalization bridges are not freshly verified here. Stage/receipt strings such as `commit` are local publication records, not Git operations.
